i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","numLines":262,"startLine":1,"totalLines":262}}}

The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into contiguous, near-equal blocks, one\n//   per MPI rank. index_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n    index_t offset = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        const index_t count = base + (static_cast<index_t>(r) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n        rowsPerRank[r] = static_cast<int>(count);\n        rowStart[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);\n        offset += count;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into contiguous, near-equal blocks, one\n//   per MPI rank. Computed identically (and independently) on every rank\n//   so no communication is required to agree on the partition.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   numRows:     total number of matrix rows\n//   numRanks:    number of MPI ranks\n//   rowsPerRank: output - number of rows assigned to each rank\n//   rowStart:    output - global index of the first row assigned to each rank\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid computeRowPartition(const index_t numRows, const int numRanks,\n                          std::vector<int>& rowsPerRank, std::vector<int>& rowStart) {\n    rowsPerRank.assign(numRanks, 0);\n    rowStart.assign(numRanks, 0);\n    const index_t base = numRows / static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n    const index_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n    index_t offset = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        const index_t count = base + (static_cast<index_t>(r) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n        rowsPerRank[r] = static_cast<int>(count);\n        rowStart[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);\n        offset += count;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (every rank receives the same argv from\n    // the MPI launcher, so this can be done independently and without\n    // communication).\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n        printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n        printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\",\n               nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n        printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Row-based partitioning: each rank owns a contiguous block of matrix\n    // rows. Computed identically on every rank, so no communication is\n    // needed to agree on it.\n    std::vector<int> rowsPerRank, rowStart;\n    computeRowPartition(numRows, numRanks, rowsPerRank, rowStart);\n    const index_t numRowsLocal = static_cast<index_t>(rowsPerRank[rank]);\n\n    // The dense vector is small (O(dim)) and read by every row regardless\n    // of which rank owns it, so it is replicated on all ranks.\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);\n\n    // Non-zero counts (and byte offsets) per rank, needed to scatter the\n    // CSR value/column arrays.\n    std::vector<int> nnzPerRank(numRanks, 0);\n    std::vector<int> nnzStart(numRanks, 0);\n\n    // Full matrix data. Only rank 0 needs to hold it (it is generated\n    // sequentially there and then distributed); other ranks leave these\n    // empty to avoid replicating O(nnz) memory across the cluster.\n    std::vector<double> h_val;\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols;\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters;\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        if (isRoot) printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n        h_val.resize(nItems);\n        h_cols.resize(nItems);\n        h_rowDelimiters.resize(numRows + 1);\n\n        fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n        fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n        initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            nnzStart[r] = static_cast<int>(h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r]]);\n            nnzPerRank[r] = static_cast<int>(h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r] + rowsPerRank[r]]) - nnzStart[r];\n        }\n\n        // For validation, compute reference solution on the full (still\n        // undistributed) matrix, matching the original serial semantics.\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n            h_reference.resize(numRows);\n            spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                    h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Distribute the dense vector and the per-rank non-zero counts.\n    MPI_Bcast(h_vec.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzPerRank.data(), numRanks, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzStart.data(), numRanks, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    const index_t nItemsLocal = static_cast<index_t>(nnzPerRank[rank]);\n\n    // Scatter the CSR value/column arrays: each rank receives exactly the\n    // non-zeros belonging to the rows it owns.\n    std::vector<double> local_val(nItemsLocal);\n    std::vector<index_t> local_cols(nItemsLocal);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_val.data() : nullptr, nnzPerRank.data(), nnzStart.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                 local_val.data(), static_cast<int>(nItemsLocal), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_cols.data() : nullptr, nnzPerRank.data(), nnzStart.data(), MPI_UINT32_T,\n                 local_cols.data(), static_cast<int>(nItemsLocal), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Scatter row-start offsets (global, absolute nnz indices) for each row\n    // owned by this rank, then rebuild a local, zero-based rowDelimiters\n    // array of size numRowsLocal + 1.\n    std::vector<index_t> local_rowDelimiters(numRowsLocal + 1);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_rowDelimiters.data() : nullptr, rowsPerRank.data(), rowStart.data(), MPI_UINT32_T,\n                 local_rowDelimiters.data(), static_cast<int>(numRowsLocal), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    local_rowDelimiters[numRowsLocal] = static_cast<index_t>(nnzStart[rank] + nnzPerRank[rank]);\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < numRowsLocal; ++i) {\n        local_rowDelimiters[i] -= static_cast<index_t>(nnzStart[rank]);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> local_out(numRowsLocal);\n    std::vector<double> h_out;\n    if (isRoot) h_out.resize(numRows);\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation. Since the dense vector never changes\n    // between iterations, no inter-rank communication is required inside\n    // the timed loop, only local computation on each rank's row block.\n    if (isRoot) printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(local_val.data(), local_cols.data(), local_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRowsLocal, local_out.data());\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    // Gather the per-rank partial outputs into the full result vector on\n    // rank 0, only once after the timed loop (the intermediate values of\n    // earlier iterations are never observed, matching the original code).\n    MPI_Gatherv(local_out.data(), static_cast<int>(numRowsLocal), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                isRoot ? h_out.data() : nullptr, rowsPerRank.data(), rowStart.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    bool valid = true;\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate performance metrics\n        const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n\n        printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n            printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", valid ? \"PASSED\" : \"FAILED\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_CXX_BOOL, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        exitCode = valid ? 0 : 1;\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":270,"oldLines":7,"newStart":270,"newLines":7,"lines":["     std::vector<double> h_reference;"," ","     if (isRoot) {","-        if (isRoot) printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");","+        printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");","         h_val.resize(nItems);","         h_cols.resize(nItems);","         h_rowDelimiters.resize(numRows + 1);"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into contiguous, near-equal blocks, one\n//   per MPI rank. Computed identically (and independently) on every rank\n//   so no communication is required to agree on the partition.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   numRows:     total number of matrix rows\n//   numRanks:    number of MPI ranks\n//   rowsPerRank: output - number of rows assigned to each rank\n//   rowStart:    output - global index of the first row assigned to each rank\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid computeRowPartition(const index_t numRows, const int numRanks,\n                          std::vector<int>& rowsPerRank, std::vector<int>& rowStart) {\n    rowsPerRank.assign(numRanks, 0);\n    rowStart.assign(numRanks, 0);\n    const index_t base = numRows / static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n    const index_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n    index_t offset = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        const index_t count = base + (static_cast<index_t>(r) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n        rowsPerRank[r] = static_cast<int>(count);\n        rowStart[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);\n        offset += count;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (every rank receives the same argv from\n    // the MPI launcher, so this can be done independently and without\n    // communication).\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n        printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n        printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\",\n               nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n        printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Row-based partitioning: each rank owns a contiguous block of matrix\n    // rows. Computed identically on every rank, so no communication is\n    // needed to agree on it.\n    std::vector<int> rowsPerRank, rowStart;\n    computeRowPartition(numRows, numRanks, rowsPerRank, rowStart);\n    const index_t numRowsLocal = static_cast<index_t>(rowsPerRank[rank]);\n\n    // The dense vector is small (O(dim)) and read by every row regardless\n    // of which rank owns it, so it is replicated on all ranks.\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);\n\n    // Non-zero counts (and byte offsets) per rank, needed to scatter the\n    // CSR value/column arrays.\n    std::vector<int> nnzPerRank(numRanks, 0);\n    std::vector<int> nnzStart(numRanks, 0);\n\n    // Full matrix data. Only rank 0 needs to hold it (it is generated\n    // sequentially there and then distributed); other ranks leave these\n    // empty to avoid replicating O(nnz) memory across the cluster.\n    std::vector<double> h_val;\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols;\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters;\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Initializing data static_cast<int>(h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r]]);\n            nnzPerRank[r] = static_cast<int>(h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r] + rowsPerRank[r]]) - nnzStart[r];\n        }\n\n        // For validation, compute reference solution on the full (still\n        // undistributed) matrix, matching the original serial semantics.\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n            h_reference.resize(numRows);\n            spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                    h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Distribute the dense vector and the per-rank non-zero counts.\n    MPI_Bcast(h_vec.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzPerRank.data(), numRanks, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzStart.data(), numRanks, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    const index_t nItemsLocal = static_cast<index_t>(nnzPerRank[rank]);\n\n    // Scatter the CSR value/column arrays: each rank receives exactly the\n    // non-zeros belonging to the rows it owns.\n    std::vector<double> local_cols.data(), static_cast<int>(nItemsLocal), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Scatter row-start offsets (global, absolute nnz indices) for each row\n    // owned by this rank, then rebuild a local, zero-based rowDelimiters\n    // array of size numRowsLocal + 1.\n    std::vector<index_t> local_rowDelimiters(numRowsLocal + 1);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_rowDelimiters.data() : static_cast<index_t>(nnzStart[rank]);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> local_out(numRowsLocal);\n    std::vector<double> h_out;\n    if (isRoot) h_out.resize(numRows);\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation. Since the dense vector never changes\n    // between iterations, no inter-rank communication is required inside\n    // the timed loop, only local computation on each rank's row block.\n    if (isRoot) printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(local_val.data(), local_cols.data(), local_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRowsLocal, local_out.data());\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    // Gather the per-rank partial outputs into the full result vector on\n    // rank 0, only once after the timed loop (the intermediate values of\n    // earlier iterations are never observed, matching the original code).\n    MPI_Gatherv(local_out.data(), static_cast<int>(numRowsLocal), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                isRoot ? i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into contiguous, near-equal blocks, one\n//   per MPI rank. Computed identically (and independently) on every rank\n//   so no communication is required to agree on the partition.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   numRows:     total number of matrix rows\n//   numRanks:    number of MPI ranks\n//   rowsPerRank: output - number of rows assigned to each rank\n//   rowStart:    output - global index of the first row assigned to each rank\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid computeRowPartition(const index_t numRows, const int numRanks,\n                          std::vector<int>& rowsPerRank, std::vector<int>& rowStart) {\n    rowsPerRank.assign(numRanks, 0);\n    rowStart.assign(numRanks, 0);\n    const index_t base = numRows / static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n    const index_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<index_t>(numRanks);\n    index_t offset = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        const index_t count = base + (static_cast<index_t>(r) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n        rowsPerRank[r] = static_cast<int>(count);\n        rowStart[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);\n        offset += count;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (every rank receives the same argv from\n    // the MPI launcher, so this can be done independently and without\n    // communication).\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n        printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n        printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\",\n               nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n        printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Row-based partitioning: each rank owns a contiguous block of matrix\n    // rows. Computed identically on every rank, so no communication is\n    // needed to agree on it.\n    std::vector<int> rowsPerRank, rowStart;\n    computeRowPartition(numRows, numRanks, rowsPerRank, rowStart);\n    const index_t numRowsLocal = static_cast<index_t>(rowsPerRank[rank]);\n\n    // The dense vector is small (O(dim)) and read by every row regardless\n    // of which rank owns it, so it is replicated on all ranks.\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);\n\n    // Non-zero counts (and byte offsets) per rank, needed to scatter the\n    // CSR value/column arrays.\n    std::vector<int> nnzPerRank(numRanks, 0);\n    std::vector<int> nnzStart(numRanks, 0);\n\n    // Full matrix data. Only rank 0 needs to hold it (it is generated\n    // sequentially there and then distributed); other ranks leave these\n    // empty to avoid replicating O(nnz) memory across the cluster.\n    std::vector<double> h_val;\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols;\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters;\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n        h_val.resize(nItems);\n        h_cols.resize(nItems);\n        h_rowDelimiters.resize(numRows + 1);\n\n        fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n        fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n        initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            nnzStart[r] = static_cast<int>(h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r]]);\n            nnzPerRank[r] = static_cast<int>(h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r] + rowsPerRank[r]]) - nnzStart[r];\n        }\n\n        // For validation, compute reference solution on the full (still\n        // undistributed) matrix, matching the original serial semantics.\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n            h_reference.resize(numRows);\n            spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                    h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Distribute the dense vector and the per-rank non-zero counts.\n    MPI_Bcast(h_vec.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzPerRank.data(), numRanks, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzStart.data(), numRanks, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    const index_t nItemsLocal = static_cast<index_t>(nnzPerRank[rank]);\n\n    // Scatter the CSR value/column arrays: each rank receives exactly the\n    // non-zeros belonging to the rows it owns.\n    std::vector<double> local_val(nItemsLocal);\n    std::vector<index_t> local_cols(nItemsLocal);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_val.data() : nullptr, nnzPerRank.data(), nnzStart.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                 local_val.data(), static_cast<int>(nItemsLocal), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_cols.data() : nullptr, nnzPerRank.data(), nnzStart.data(), MPI_UINT32_T,\n                 local_cols.data(), static_cast<int>(nItemsLocal), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Scatter row-start offsets (global, absolute nnz indices) for each row\n    // owned by this rank, then rebuild a local, zero-based rowDelimiters\n    // array of size numRowsLocal + 1.\n    std::vector<index_t> local_rowDelimiters(numRowsLocal + 1);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_rowDelimiters.data() : nullptr, rowsPerRank.data(), rowStart.data(), MPI_UINT32_T,\n                 local_rowDelimiters.data(), static_cast<int>(numRowsLocal), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    local_rowDelimiters[numRowsLocal] = static_cast<index_t>(nnzStart[rank] + nnzPerRank[rank]);\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < numRowsLocal; ++i) {\n        local_rowDelimiters[i] -= static_cast<index_t>(nnzStart[rank]);\n    }\n    fprintf(stderr, \"[rank %d] numRowsLocal=%u nItemsLocal=%u nnzStart=%d nnzPerRank=%d rowStart=%d rowsPerRank=%d delim0=%u delimLast=%u\\n\",\n            rank, numRowsLocal, nItemsLocal, nnzStart[rank], nnzPerRank[rank], rowStart[rank], rowsPerRank[rank],\n            local_rowDelimiters[0], local_rowDelimiters[numRowsLocal]);\n    fflush(stderr);\n\n    std::vector<double> local_out(numRowsLocal);\n    std::vector<double> h_out;\n    if (isRoot) h_out.resize(numRows);\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation. Since the dense vector never changes\n    // between iterations, no inter-rank communication is required inside\n    // the timed loop, only local computation on each rank's row block.\n    if (isRoot) printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(local_val.data(), local_cols.data(), local_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRowsLocal, local_out.data());\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    // Gather the per-rank partial outputs into the full result vector on\n    // rank 0, only once after the timed loop (the intermediate values of\n    // earlier iterations are never observed, matching the original code).\n    MPI_Gatherv(local_out.data(), static_cast<int>(numRowsLocal), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                isRoot ? h_out.data() : nullptr, rowsPerRank.data(), rowStart.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    bool valid = true;\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate performance metrics\n        const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n\n        printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n            printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", valid ? \"PASSED\" : \"FAILED\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_CXX_BOOL, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        exitCode = valid ? 0 : 1;\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":316,"oldLines":14,"newStart":316,"newLines":10,"lines":["     std::vector<index_t> local_rowDelimiters(numRowsLocal + 1);","     MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? Computed identically on every rank, so no communication is\n    // needed to agree on it.\n    std::vector<int> rowsPerRank, rowStart;\n    computeRowPartition(numRows, numRanks, rowsPerRank, rowStart);\n    const index_t numRowsLocal = static_cast<index_t>(rowsPerRank[rank]);\n\n    // The dense vector is small (O(dim)) and read by every row regardless\n    // of which rank owns it, so it is replicated on all ranks.\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);\n\n    // Non-zero counts (and byte offsets) per rank, needed to scatter the\n    // CSR value/column arrays.\n    std::vector<int> nnzPerRank(numRanks, 0);\n    std::vector<int> nnzStart(numRanks, 0);\n\n    // Full matrix data. Only rank 0 needs to hold it (it is generated\n    // sequentially there and then distributed); other ranks leave these\n    // empty to avoid replicating O(nnz) memory across the cluster.\n    std::vector<double> h_val;\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols;\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters;\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n        h_val.resize(nItems);\n        h_cols.resize(nItems);\n        h_rowDelimiters.resize(numRows + 1);\n\n        fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n        fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n        initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            nnzStart[r] = static_cast<int>(h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r]]);\n            nnzPerRank[r] = static_cast<int>(h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r] + rowsPerRank[r]]) - nnzStart[r];\n        }\n\n        // For validation, compute reference solution on the full (still\n        // undistributed) matrix, matching the original serial semantics.\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n            h_reference.resize(numRows);\n            spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                    h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Distribute the dense vector and the per-rank non-zero counts.\n    MPI_Bcast(h_vec.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzPerRank.data(), numRanks, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzStart.data(), numRanks, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    const index_t nItemsLocal = static_cast<index_t>(nnzPerRank[rank]);\n\n    // Scatter the CSR value/column arrays: each rank receives exactly the\n    // non-zeros belonging to the rows it owns.\n    std::vector<double> local_val(nItemsLocal);\n    std::vector<index_t> local_cols(nItemsLocal);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_val.data() : nullptr, nnzPerRank.data(), nnzStart.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                 local_val.data(), static_cast<int>(nItemsLocal), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Scatterv(isRoot ? h_cols.data() : nullptr, nnzPerRank.data(), nnzStart.data(), MPI_UINT32_T,\n                 local_cols.data(), static_cast<int>(nItemsLocal), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Scatter row-start offsets (global, absolute nnz indices) for each row\n    // owned by this rank, then rebuild a local, zero-based rowDelimiters","numLines":130,"startLine":185,"totalLines":384}}}
